961 entries, schema-migration to scout. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **schema-migration**: Use for any change to db/01-schema.sql, a Laravel migration, or a SQL view, and for the ADR that has to travel with it. Highest-stakes work in the repository: five people share one database, the constraints encode product requirements, and a view that answers the wrong question is invisible until someone reads a chart wrong. Also use to diagnose a MySQL error nobody recognises. ([theintonerzero/cse3cap](https://github.com/theintonerzero/cse3cap/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/schema-migration.md) · ★2)
- **schema-migration-auditor**: Read-only auditor that detects Python (Pydantic v2) ↔ Rust (serde) schema drift before it becomes a serialization failure or silent data loss: missing / extra fields, type mismatches (f64↔float, Vec↔List, Option↔Optional, HashMap↔Dict), required-vs-optional skew, and serialization-name disagreement where a Pydantic `Field(alias=...)` does not match the Rust `serde(rename=...)` on the same wire field. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/schiedsverfahren-adr/agents/researcher.md) · ★61)
- **schiedsverfahren-adr-reviewer**: Du bist die **Qualitäts- und Risikostufe** vor Auslieferung an die mandatsführende Anwältin bzw. den mandatsführenden Anwalt. Du erstellst keinen neuen Inhalt — du **prüfst** den Drafter-Entwurf gegen sechs Kategorien und gibst einen Pass/Fix-Befund. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/schiedsverfahren-adr/agents/reviewer.md) · ★61)
- **schism-historian**: Schism & Sectarian-Formation Historian. Standalone agent (panel member #12). Maker + reviewer. Tracks how a reading of a text fractured the Church — councils, schisms, new sects, and movements whose Christian identity is now contested. ([chenders/BibleStudyAsAllegory](https://github.com/chenders/BibleStudyAsAllegory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/schism-historian.md) · ★1)
- **schizophrenic**: When to use: need to produce a High-Level Execution Plan. Valid inputs: requirement = user's original requirement or the AI's feedback; plan_file_path = prior plan from last round; preference_path = preferences doc path; forbidden_path = forbidden items doc path. ([zhaoyshine/looppower](https://github.com/zhaoyshine/looppower/blob/HEAD/agents/schizophrenic.md))
- **schizophrenic**: 何时使用：需要产出一份 High-Level 执行计划时。 接受的输入：requirement = 用户原始需求或 AI 的反馈；plan_file_path = 上一轮产出的计划；preference_path = 偏好文档路径；forbidden_path = 禁止事项文档路径。 ([zhaoyshine/looppower](https://github.com/zhaoyshine/looppower/blob/HEAD/zh/agents/schizophrenic.md))
- **schluss-pruefer**: Beurteilt Schlussformel, Zeugnisabreden und Statuswechsel: welche Signalwirkung der Schluss hat, und was davon überhaupt verlangt werden kann. Nutze diesen Agenten in beiden Strecken, eine Instanz je Fall. Er formuliert keine Schlussformel. ([MakeNative/zeugnis-agent-kit](https://github.com/MakeNative/zeugnis-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/schluss-pruefer.md))
- **schnitt-editor**: Schnitt-Editor / Cutter (Montage & Pacing) eines Elite-Videostudios — GETRENNT von einem Motion-Spezialisten (Montage/Rhythmus ≠ Grafik/Titel). MUST BE USED PROACTIVELY für die finale Montage: Timeline/EDL, Cut-Rate je Säule (Cuts/min-Korridor), harter vs. weicher Schnitt, Schnitt auf musikalische Phrase, Quiz-Denk-Pause OHNE Schnitt, Gesamtrhythmus/Fluss über ALLE Clips (»Picture Lock« VOR Ton-Finalisierung). Misst die Cut-Rate selbst (ffmpeg scene-detect). Trigger: "schnitt &lt;folge&gt;", "montage &lt;x&gt;", "pacing &lt;folge&gt;", "edl &lt;projekt&gt;", "cut-rate &lt;x&gt;", "picture lock", "schnitt-editor". ([NEO849/a-team-2](https://github.com/NEO849/a-team-2/blob/HEAD/agents/schnitt-editor.md))
- **scholar**: Standalone academic paper research specialist. Searches both PubMed and bioRxiv for a given topic, returns a clean structured list of results, and supports follow-up actions (download open-access full text, save as markdown, deeper synthesis). NOTE: The /ideation command performs searches inline — it does NOT spawn this agent as a sub-agent. Use this agent only for ad-hoc literature searches outside the ideation workflow. ([stanislavjiricek/neuroflow](https://github.com/stanislavjiricek/neuroflow/blob/HEAD/agents/scholar.md) · ★8)
- **scholar**: Conducts academic research and coordinates science — literature search/synthesis via OpenAlex and academic writing, citation-network analysis, adversarial methodology critique (ROBUST/WEAK/INVALID), and cross-domain STEM coordination. Use for scholarly research, citation analysis, methodology review, or multi-discipline science questions. Modes: scholarship, citation-graph, methodology, science-coord. Set metadata.mode. NOT for: market/business research (use market-research-analyst) or social-science discipline analysis (use social-scientist). - **scholar**: Deep-cognition variant of Reader for dense theory, foundational papers, and hard texts. Same requested perspectives and workflow with stronger voices; one scholar can cover multiple lenses. Reader handles routine reads. - **scholar**: Use this agent to find, fetch and weigh the primary sources behind a claim: ancient, medieval or modern texts, translations, editions, observations and their numbers. It returns verbatim quotations with their passage, translator, link and standing, says what was observed and what is schematic, and says where the sources stop. - **scholar**: Deep research scholar retrieving and synthesizing knowledge ([ngdletrung/JKAI-Zenith](https://github.com/ngdletrung/JKAI-Zenith/blob/HEAD/intelligence/agents/agent_scholar.md))
- **scholar**: Pesquisa pergunta técnica em fontes externas primárias (docs oficiais, specs, changelogs) e devolve achados com links. Use para fato fora do repo. Perguntas sobre o próprio Claude Code, hooks ou SDK da Anthropic vão para claude-code-guide quando ele existir na sessão; sem ele (por exemplo, sob o orquestrador), vêm para cá. Não edita arquivos. - **scholar**: Establish a fact that is expensive to derive, and cite what proves it. Returns the fact with its evidence, or says plainly that it could not be established. Use when implementing a new external QM code end-to-end: C++ calculator class, settings, file parsers, module registration in scine_utilities, then Puffin program class and job class in scine_puffin. Trigger phrases: add calculator, new QM calculator, implement calculator, new software calculator, add QM code. ([rangsimanketkaew/scine-agent-skills](https://github.com/rangsimanketkaew/scine-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/scine-add-calculator.agent.md) · ★3)
- **scine-add-calculator-only-puffin**: Add a new quantum chemistry (QM) software as a job in Puffin (Python) only — without touching the Utils C++ side. Use when the Core::Calculator already exists in scine_utilities and you only need the Puffin program class, config registration, and job class. Trigger phrases: add calculator puffin, puffin job only, puffin calculator, new puffin job, scine_puffin calculator, add job puffin. ([rangsimanketkaew/scine-agent-skills](https://github.com/rangsimanketkaew/scine-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/scine-add-calculator-only-puffin.agent.md) · ★3)
- **scine-add-calculator-only-utils**: Add a new quantum chemistry (QM) software as a calculator in SCINE Utils (C++) only — without the Puffin Python side. Use when you only need the C++ Core::Calculator implementation: class hierarchy, settings, input file creator, output parser, helper, module registration, and CMake. Trigger phrases: add calculator utils, utils calculator only, implement C++ calculator, new calculator utils, scine_utilities calculator.
